`types.ts` — Type definitions (`PluginSDKType`, `PluginContext`)\n\n**Shared SDK Types** (`/app/plugins/sdk.ts`):\n- Exported from all plugins\n- Defines `PluginContext`, `PluginObject`, `PluginSDKType`, `getSDK()` helper\n\n---\n\n### 4. **Other Plugins**\n\n- `/app/plugins/warehouse/` — Warehouse management (CRUD via `thisPlugin.objects`, type=\"warehouse\")\n- The warehouse service began hourly/nightly full inventory sync, replacing on-demand lookups with bulk polling of the products API.\n\n2. **10:00–11:45 UTC** — Warehouse DB query rate spiked from ~42 qpm to ~1700 qpm (40x increase). The host-app's shared PostgreSQL connection pool usage climbed from ~34% to ~85%, approaching saturation.\n\n3. **~11:50 UTC** — DB contention reached a threshold where ai-description's product lookup queries (`GET /api/products/{id}`) began experiencing db_wait times of 2500–2700ms (normally negligible). ai-description p99 latency started rising.\n\n4. **~12:05 UTC** — LiteLLM proxy latency also degraded (p99 from ~1100ms to ~4000ms) as a secondary effect of shared resource contention, despite constant token throughput (~6200 tpm).\n\n5. **~13:00 UTC** — Latency stabilized at degraded levels (~5800–6000ms p99), with connection pool usage plateaued at ~85%.\n\n## Evidence\n\n| Signal | Before (08:00–10:00) | After (12:00–14:00) | Source |\n|--------|---------------------|---------------------|--------|\n| ai-description p99 latency | ~1800ms | ~5800ms | aj-metrics |\n| ai-description llm_call_latency p99 | ~1000ms | ~3800ms | aj-metrics |\n| warehouse db_query_rate (p50) | ~42 qpm | ~1700 qpm | aj-metrics |\n| host-app db_connection_pool_usage (p50) | ~34% | ~83% | aj-metrics |\n| litellm-proxy p99 latency | ~1100ms | ~4000ms | aj-metrics |\n| litellm-proxy token_rate | ~6200 tpm | ~6200 tpm (unchanged) | aj-metrics |\n| DB wait in trace spans | — | 2500–2700ms | aj-traces (t-001, t-002) |\n\n## Mitigation applied\n\nDisabled the `warehouse-full-inventory-sync` feature gate at ~14:00 UTC:\n\n```\nset_feature_gate_state(name=\"warehouse-full-inventory-sync\", state=\"off\")\n→ revision: rev-auto-3ee1bb0a\n```\n\nThis stops the warehouse service's bulk product API polling, which was the source of the DB contention.\n\n## Notification\n\n**The warehouse-product team (p.warehouse@example.com) must be notified.** They authored the change (rev-001) and own the feature gate. Before re-enabling `warehouse-full-inventory-sync`, the team should:\n\n- Implement rate limiting or batching for the inventory sync to avoid saturating the shared connection pool.\n- Consider using a read replica or dedicated connection pool for bulk sync operations.\n- Coordinate with the platform team on acceptable query rate budgets for the shared PostgreSQL instance.\n\n## What was NOT the cause\n\n- **ai-description v2.2 deploy (rev-002, 2026-04-16 20:00 UTC):** The lazy-cache refactor deployed the day before and ran without incident for 14+ hours before degradation began. Metrics were stable all morning.\n- **ai-description-lazy-cache feature gate:** Active but unrelated; the degradation timeline does not correlate with this gate.\n- **LLM model performance:** Token throughput was constant; LLM proxy latency increase was a secondary effect of resource contention, not an upstream model issue.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
